# Agents · M · part 5

934 entries, mechanic to memory-search. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **mechanic**: Applies edits and runs commands that the brief spells out exactly. Renames with given old and new names, version bumps, find and replace, moving files, applying one proven recipe to each file in a list, regenerating a file with a given command. Called by foreman dispatch for mechanical subtasks (the fan-out step of the migration and refactor templates). Never designs, improvises a different change, touches files outside its owns list, or commits. ([Sagargupta16/foreman](https://github.com/Sagargupta16/foreman/blob/HEAD/agents/mechanic.md) · ★1)
- **mechanic**: Cheap, mechanical work with one right answer — renames, lint fixes, link fixes, catalog sync, regenerating indexes, log triage, doc-drift checks against code. Use instead of a stronger model whenever the task needs no judgment. ([3olive3/atelier-catalog](https://github.com/3olive3/atelier-catalog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mechanic.md))
- **mechanic**: Perform simple mechanical repository edits such as renames, boilerplate, formatting-adjacent changes and small docs updates. ([ai-development-harness/ai-development-harness-template](https://github.com/ai-development-harness/ai-development-harness-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mechanic.md))
- **mechanic**: Apply mechanical edits from an exact map in the assignment (renames, reference updates, manifest entries) and verify with grep. Stops on anything needing a decision. No commits. ([BATUSystems/skillsync](https://github.com/BATUSystems/skillsync/blob/HEAD/library/agents/mechanic.md))
- **mechanic**: Low-risk mechanical edits, pinned to the cheapest capable tier. Delegate here only when the change is already fully specified and needs no design judgment (applying a decided rename across files, fixing formatting or typos, updating docs/comments to match a stated change, or making the same small edit in many places). Runs on the cheapest tier to keep routine edits off the main session's budget. NOT for anything requiring architectural decisions, ambiguous requirements, or a fix whose approach isn't yet settled; send those to the planner agent or the main session first. ([blineadam/agent-plan-and-track](https://github.com/blineadam/agent-plan-and-track/blob/HEAD/agents/mechanic.md))
- **mechanic**: Cheap fast model for mechanical, low-judgment work - batch renames, applying a precisely specified edit across many files, generating boilerplate from an exact template, formatting sweeps. The spec must be complete; this agent must never design or decide. ([Coach-Foundation/claude-code-best-practices](https://github.com/Coach-Foundation/claude-code-best-practices/blob/HEAD/plugins/ap-optimal-claude/agents/mechanic.md))
- **mechanic**: Applies one mechanical change that can be stated as a rule across a known set of files: a rename, a moved import, a config key changed everywhere, a generated file refreshed. Use when the orchestrator can write the change as before and after; not for anything that needs design judgment. ([devincii-io/cmp-bare](https://github.com/devincii-io/cmp-bare/blob/HEAD/plugins/acme-dev/agents/mechanic.md))
- **mechanic**: Fast, cheap mechanical edits with no design judgment: renames, formatting, moving files, updating imports, fixing typos, regenerating indexes. Refuses anything that needs a decision. ([DiegoOrtizS/dev-kit](https://github.com/DiegoOrtizS/dev-kit/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/mechanic.md))
- **mechanic**: Small tier. Does mechanical work from a precise brief: digging through CI logs, resolving simple conflicts by union, status and doc edits, and waiting on checks. Reports briefly; no design decisions. ([fivvi-app/fivvi](https://github.com/fivvi-app/fivvi/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/mechanic.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **mechanic**: Haiku Low worker for mechanical work - apply a specified patch, draft docs from a spec, generate fixtures, or execute an exact list of checks/commands and report real output. À invoquer après une décision d'architecture, une contrainte ou un piège non évident, un changement de schéma de données ou de contrat partagé, un changement de vocabulaire du domaine, ou l'apparition ou la disparition d'un sous-système. Ne pas invoquer pour l'ajout d'un fichier dont le rôle se devine par son nom et son chemin, ni pour un changement cosmétique. ([blaisebarrette/Plugin-Claude-Code---Sub-agents](https://github.com/blaisebarrette/Plugin-Claude-Code---Sub-agents/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/memoire-projet.md) · ★1)

Use it before re-deriving anything non-trivial ("why is it built this way?", "have we hit this before, here or in another project?", "what should I know before changing X?"), or when an injected memory pointer looks relevant but needs digging. Read-only; it never changes the graph. ([StuCM/claude-memory-graph](https://github.com/StuCM/claude-memory-graph/blob/HEAD/agents/memory-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **memory-researcher**: Research worker (Haiku) that answers a question from the owner's memory first - notes, memories and, when needed, the papers, documents and syntheses behind them - and returns short conclusions with their references; when the memory does not cover the question it researches outside, records what it found as findings, and says which parts of its answer are new and unchecked. Use when a question needs more than one search of the memory, when the answer may need sources or outside research, or before a decision that should rest on what is already known, so the main model gets conclusions instead… ([Mili-ssm/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/Mili-ssm/Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/memory/agents/memory-researcher.md))
- **memory-resource**: You are a memory and resource specialist focused on **allocation patterns, leaks, unnecessary retention, buffer/stream usage, and resource handle lifecycle**. Your job is to find the allocations and retentions the program doesn't need. ([quinnjr/quinn-skills](https://github.com/quinnjr/quinn-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/quinn-skills/skills/optimize/agents/memory-resource.md))
- **memory-resource-analyzer**: Use this agent to find memory leaks, retain cycles, resource exhaustion, and unbounded growth patterns in changed code. &lt;example&gt; Context: A PR adds event listeners in a React component. user: "Check this for memory issues" assistant: "I'll use the memory-resource-analyzer to check for listener leaks and retain cycles." &lt;commentary&gt; Event listeners added without cleanup in React cause memory leaks on unmount. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([bradwindy/ultimate-code-review](https://github.com/bradwindy/ultimate-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/memory-resource-analyzer.md) · ★4)
- **memory-retention-and-erasure-engineer**: Forgetting, consolidation and erasure for a durable memory store: TTL and size caps, write-path vs offline consolidation, contradiction and staleness, and what survives a delete (versions, embeddings, summaries). NOT a legal/DSAR determination → data-governance-privacy. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/memory-engineering/agents/memory-retention-and-erasure-engineer.md) · ★9)
- **memory-retriever**: Queries compressed project summaries and returns relevant past context. Use when needing project history, past decisions, or context for current work. Supports filtering by namespace, keyword, date, and file path. ([kronus-tech/kronus](https://github.com/kronus-tech/kronus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/memory-retriever.md) · ★4)
- **memory-retriever**: Background agent that retrieves and synthesizes relevant memories from the database. Queries the memories table by project context, filters deprecated/low-confidence entries, and returns a concise markdown summary. ([MattVOLTA/outworkos_diy](https://github.com/MattVOLTA/outworkos_diy/blob/HEAD/skills/plugins/memory-system/agents/memory-retriever.md) · ★4)
- **memory-retriever**: ForestNav 森林路径规划项目记忆检索 Agent。从 bigmemory/ 和 .pipeline/ 检索与查询意图相关的上下文，返回精选摘要。 ([unstun/forestnav](https://github.com/unstun/forestnav/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/memory-retriever.md))
- **memory-review**: Review Remnic memories for a project or session and propose corrections or additions ([joshuaswarren/remnic](https://github.com/joshuaswarren/remnic/blob/HEAD/packages/plugin-agy/agents/memory-review.md) · ★233 · 2 copies)
- **memory-reviewer**: Reviews one commit range of this repo in a review round, for copies of large buffers, memory use and the audio callback. Read only. Use when a review round routes memory-reviewer. ([lloydmeta/stemyla](https://github.com/lloydmeta/stemyla/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/memory-reviewer.md) · ★6)
- **memory-reviewer**: Review changes to the memory package for embedding quality, note/profile correctness, and retrieval accuracy. Use when modifying internal/memory/*.go files. ([cherryroots/voltgpt](https://github.com/cherryroots/voltgpt/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/memory-reviewer.md))
- **memory-reviewer**: Reviews and curates curated-context memories. Use when the user wants to audit, clean up, or reorganize their stored project context. ([odmarkj/curated-context](https://github.com/odmarkj/curated-context/blob/HEAD/agents/memory-reviewer.md))
- **memory-safety**: Analyzes C++ code for memory safety and undefined behavior ([CarlSandstrom/OpenLoom](https://github.com/CarlSandstrom/OpenLoom/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/memory-safety.md))
- **memory-safety**: Runs ASAN and UBSan across every C suite and hunts leaks, use-after-free, and ownership violations. Use before any release and after any change to arena, pool, or interpreter lifetime code. ([Codric-Enterprise/ClaudRick](https://github.com/Codric-Enterprise/ClaudRick/blob/HEAD/%5B/.claude/agents/memory-safety.md) · 2 copies)
- **memory-safety**: Memory Safety Reviewer subagent. C++ memory leak detection, RAII compliance, heap/stack analysis, ASAN integration, RTOS memory safety. Participates in Phase A (requirements) and Phase C (verification). ([jeanboutros/tian-lu](https://github.com/jeanboutros/tian-lu/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/memory-safety.md))
- **memory-safety-auditor**: Read-mostly auditor for memory and thread safety: ASAN/UBSan sweeps of C/C++, leaks, use-after-free, and thread-safety in Python (EArchive locking) and Java. Use before releases and after lifetime changes. ([Codric-Enterprise/everlang-dpl](https://github.com/Codric-Enterprise/everlang-dpl/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/memory-safety-auditor.md))
- **memory-safety-challenger**: Challenger variant of Memory Safety Reviewer for Dual-Model Challenge. Critiques memory safety analysis and RAII compliance from an independent model perspective. Powered by glm-5.2. Invoked in Phase A (A2) and Phase C (C1). ([jeanboutros/tian-lu](https://github.com/jeanboutros/tian-lu/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/memory-safety-challenger.md))
- **memory-saver**: Writes knowledge into the user's Markdown memory base from a brief prepared by the main model. Invoked by the /save skill so that file mechanics run on a cheaper model. Use ONLY for writing memory from a brief. ([Valerii097/claude-auto-memory](https://github.com/Valerii097/claude-auto-memory/blob/HEAD/agents/memory-saver.md) · ★2)
- **memory-scientist**: Designs provenance-aware epistemic memory, RuVector/RVF adapters, retrieval tests, and anti-poisoning policy. ([ItsUkrainianCat/FORGE-RLM](https://github.com/ItsUkrainianCat/FORGE-RLM/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/memory-scientist.md))
- **memory-scout**: Search .flow/memory/ for entries relevant to the current task or request. ([gmickel/flow-next](https://github.com/gmickel/flow-next/blob/HEAD/plugins/flow-next/agents/memory-scout.md) · ★727 · 5 copies)
- **memory-scout**: Read-only memory scout — searches DB memory and raw session transcripts before style, format, or convention-sensitive work. Returns a compact verdict only. ([dmlguq456/hearting](https://github.com/dmlguq456/hearting/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/memory-scout.md) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **memory-scout**: Gathers deep background from MEMANTO before substantial work. Use when starting significant work in a repo, resuming after time away, before a refactor or architectural change, or when the conversation needs history that a single recall cannot cover. Fans out several searches and returns a short synthesized brief with provenance instead of a memory dump. ([moorcheh-ai/memanto-agent-skills](https://github.com/moorcheh-ai/memanto-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/memory-scout.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
